# Noctra Safe Rename Report

This report inventories remaining repository occurrences of `omarchy` / `Omarchy` without applying any rename. It is intended as a preflight plan for a future safe rename pass.

## Methodology

- Scanned file and directory names with `find . -path ./.git -prune -o \( -iname '*omarchy*' -o -iname '*Omarchy*' \) -print`.
- Scanned text contents with `rg -n --hidden -i "omarchy"` while excluding binary image/font payloads from line-level extraction.
- Scanned visual asset inventory with `find` for `*.svg`, `*.png`, `*.jpg`, `*.jpeg`, and `*.webp`.
- Classified risk by external coupling: commands/aliases/migrations are highest risk; visual assets are usually medium; attribution notices are intentionally retained.

## Executive Summary

- Name/path occurrences containing Omarchy terms: **347** total paths (**4** directories, **343** files).
- Text files containing Omarchy terms: **704**.
- Visual assets audited: **183** (`svg/png/jpg/jpeg/webp`).
- Visual assets still likely Omarchy-branded: **98**.

## Global Replacement Rules

| Current identifier pattern | Recommendation | User visible? | Safe to rename? | Risk | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Omarchy` | `Noctra` | yes | yes, except attribution/license text | Medium | Preserve upstream attribution in `NOTICE`/license-derived text. |
| `omarchy` command | `noctra` primary command, keep `omarchy` compatibility alias/wrapper | yes | yes, only with compatibility | Critical | Shell muscle memory, docs, scripts, and users call this directly. |
| `omarchy-*` commands | `noctra-*` primary commands, keep `omarchy-*` wrappers or aliases for at least one compatibility cycle | yes | yes, only with compatibility | Critical | Internal scripts, Hyprland bindings, systemd units, migrations, and third-party snippets invoke these names. |
| `$OMARCHY_PATH` | `$NOCTRA_PATH`, export `$OMARCHY_PATH` as compatibility alias during transition | no | yes, only with compatibility | Critical | Install, update, migrations, configs, and shell defaults depend on it. |
| `$OMARCHY_INSTALL` | `$NOCTRA_INSTALL`, export `$OMARCHY_INSTALL` during transition | no | yes, only with compatibility | Critical | Bootstrap/install scripts source this heavily. |
| `~/.local/share/omarchy` | `~/.local/share/noctra`, symlink or migration bridge from old path | no | yes, only with migration | Critical | Existing installs and migrations reference the old application directory. |
| `~/.config/omarchy` | `~/.config/noctra`, migrate/symlink old config path | partly | yes, only with migration | High | Themes, hooks, extensions, and samples reference this config namespace. |
| `~/.local/state/omarchy` | `~/.local/state/noctra`, migrate/symlink old state path | no | yes, only with migration | High | Migrations, toggles, cache/state files depend on this. |
| `omarchy.desktop` | `Noctra.desktop` or `noctra.desktop`; update SDDM session selection/migrations | yes | yes, with migration | High | Display manager session IDs are external entry points. |
| `omarchy.service` / `omarchy-*.service` / timers | `noctra.service` / `noctra-*.service`; disable old units and enable new units in migration | yes | yes, with migration | High | Systemd unit names are persisted in user state. |
| `default/sddm/omarchy` | `default/sddm/noctra`; update SDDM theme config and install scripts | yes | yes, with migration | High | Login theme path is user-visible and system configured. |
| `default/plymouth/omarchy.*` | `default/plymouth/noctra.*`; update `Theme=` and set/reset helpers | yes | yes, with initramfs/regeneration | High | Boot splash requires system update hooks. |
| `omarchy` wallpapers/assets | Replace artwork/name with Noctra equivalents; regenerate previews/unlock images | yes | yes | Medium | Visual brand change; low code risk but high design visibility. |
| Historical migrations mentioning Omarchy | Keep as-is unless writing explicit compatibility migration | no | no | Critical | Old migrations are archival compatibility logic. |

## Subsystem Findings

### SDDM assets

- `default/sddm/omarchy/` is the remaining SDDM theme namespace and should become `default/sddm/noctra/` with a compatibility migration for the configured SDDM theme.
- Raster files under that directory are user-visible login assets and are still Omarchy-branded by location and theme role: `bullet.png`, `entry.png`, `entry-failed.png`, `lock.png`, `lock-failed.png`, and `logo.png`.

### Plymouth assets

- `default/plymouth/omarchy.plymouth` and `default/plymouth/omarchy.script` should become `noctra.plymouth` and `noctra.script`.
- `default/plymouth/logo.png`, `lock.png`, `entry.png`, `bullet.png`, `progress_bar.png`, `progress_box.png`, `preview-unlock.png`, and `logos/oma.png` should be visually replaced or audited; the `oma` short name is a remaining Omarchy abbreviation.

### Waybar assets and config

- Waybar content references are primarily command/path references (`omarchy-refresh-waybar`, `omarchy-weather-status`, `$OMARCHY_PATH/default/waybar/weather.sh`) rather than standalone image assets.
- Rename only after command wrappers and `$NOCTRA_PATH` compatibility are in place.

### Hyprland assets and config

- Hyprland references include `default/hypr/omarchy.lua`, `$OMARCHY_PATH/default/hypr/*.lua`, keybindings that execute `omarchy-*` commands, and toggle state under `~/.local/state/omarchy`.
- Rename with compatibility exports and symlinks so installed configs continue loading during migration.

### Wallpapers

- Wallpaper files whose filename contains `omarchy` are all listed in the asset inventory and should be renamed to `noctra` equivalents after artwork replacement.
- Theme preview/unlock images should be regenerated after wallpaper and boot/login artwork changes.

### Icons, SVG, PNG, JPG, WEBP

- Root `logo.svg`, root `icon.png`, Plymouth/SDDM logos, and files named `omarchy*.png` are the highest-priority visible brand assets.
- No `*.webp` files were found in the current repository scan.
- Non-Omarchy third-party application icons under `applications/icons/` should not be renamed solely for the Noctra rebrand.

## Critical Rename Order Recommendation

1. Add compatibility layer first: `omarchy` and `omarchy-*` wrappers/aliases that call `noctra`/`noctra-*`, plus `$OMARCHY_*` compatibility exports.
2. Migrate filesystem locations with symlinks: `~/.local/share/omarchy`, `~/.config/omarchy`, and `~/.local/state/omarchy`.
3. Rename internal command references in scripts/configs after wrappers exist.
4. Rename systemd units and session entries with explicit migration scripts.
5. Rename SDDM/Plymouth theme directories/descriptors and regenerate boot assets/initramfs where required.
6. Replace visual brand assets and regenerate previews last.


## First Safe Rename Pass Status

The first safe user-facing rename pass has been applied and summarized in `NOCTRA_SAFE_RENAME_PASS_SUMMARY.md`.

This pass kept high-risk and critical compatibility identifiers intact, including inherited `omarchy` commands, paths, state directories, migrations, package infrastructure, and service/session file names. It also confirmed that final bitmap/logo replacements are still required and deferred to a later asset pass because final Noctra artwork was not provided.
